- `dllseg_shape(from, bound, prev, cur)` is a segment from `from` to `bound` where `prev` is the predecessor of `cur`

- Start = head_node, head_node->prev = 0

- yp = tail_node, y = tail_node
- yp->next = y => tail_node->next = tail_node (self-loop!)
- y not in segment (tail_node is the endpoint, excluded)

So the full expansion of dllseg_shape(head_node, 0, tail_node, tail_node) does satisfy yp->next == y if tail_node->next == tail_node. But that's only true if tail_node has a self-loop.

Actually, looking at the dlistrep definition:
dlistrep_shape(l, p) = (l == 0 && emp) || exists t, data_at(field_addr(l, next), struct list*, t) * data_at(field_addr(l, prev), struct list*, p) * dlistrep_shape(t, l)

And dllseg_shape:
dllseg_shape(x, xp, yp, y) = (x == y && xp == yp && emp) || exists z, data_at(field_addr(x, next), struct list*, z) * data_at(field_addr(x, prev), struct list*, xp) * dllseg_shape(z, x, yp, y)

For dllseg_shape(head_node, 0, tail_node_prev, tail_node) * dlistrep_shape(tail_node, tail_node_prev):
= data_at(head_node->next, ..., z) * data_at(head_node->prev, ..., 0) * data_at(z->next, ..., z2) * data_at(z->prev, ..., head_node) * ... * data_at(tail_node_prev->next, ..., tail_node) * data_at(tail_node_prev->prev, ..., prev) * dlistrep_shape(tail_node, tail_node_prev)

Expanding dlistrep_shape(tail_node, tail_node_prev):
= data_at(tail_node->next, ..., y_296) * data_at(tail_node->prev, ..., tail_node_prev) * dlistrep_shape(y_296, tail_node)

So the combined expansion is:
data_at(head_node->next, ..., z) * data_at(head_node->prev, ..., 0) * ... * dlistrep_shape(y_296, tail_node)

And dlistrep_shape(head_node, 0) would unfold to:
data_at(head_node->next, ..., z) * data_at(head_node->prev, ..., 0) * dlistrep_shape(z, head_node)

And dlistrep_shape(z, head_node) = ... heading toward tail_node ... eventually:
data_at(tail_node->next, ..., y_296) * data_at(tail_node->prev, ..., tail_node_prev) * dlistrep_shape(y_296, tail_node)

So the SEPARATE expansion of dlistrep_shape(head_node, 0) would produce the same data_at nodes as the combined dllseg + dlistrep. They're EQUIVALENT!

So the strategy needs to:
1. Recognize dllseg_shape(head_node,0,tail_node_prev,tail_node) * dlistrep_shape(tail_node, tail_node_prev) = dlistrep_shape(head_node, 0)
2. Fold into dlistrep_shape(head_node, 0)

dlistrep_shape (s, r) * dlistrep_shape (q, p) | - dlistrep_shape (x, 0)

But the crash happens AFTER has_permission(&head) is processed. The processing of `&head` doesn't crash (as we saw in the non-crashing version with has_permission(&head) + has_permission(&tail)).

The crash is specifically from having BOTH undef_data_at for struct list* (from has_permission(&p)) AND undef_data_at for struct list** (from has_permission(&head/tail)) in the same invariant.

This is a type-conflict bug in QCP's invariant processing.
